📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: PLoS ONE  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-09-23  |  DOI: 10.1371/journal.pone.0345354  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 21, 9, e0345354

在野生水禽-家禽界面上高致病性禽流感(高致病性禽流感)H5N1的时空聚集:美国媒介特定的跨物种溢出风险,2022–2025年

Spatiotemporal clustering of highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) H5N1 at the wild waterfowl-poultry interface: Vector-specific spillover risks in the U.S., 2022–2025
作者： Csaba Varga
摘要中文翻译2022年2月起,北美地区出现高致病性禽流感(高致病性禽流感)H5N1的2.3.4.4b分支,凸显了感染风险的动态性、不可预测性和区域性差异。需要开展研究,评估高致病性禽流感H5在野生水禽与商业家禽交界处的时空聚集情况,以更好地理解并缓解这一风险。分析了2022年1月至2026年1月期间,北美地区在野生鸟类和商业家禽中公开的高致病性禽流感H5检测数据,按县进行分析。采用回顾性时空置换模型来识别和扫描检测率高于预期的聚集区域。在野生鸟类中,共报告了17,091例高致病性禽流感H5检测,涉及1,467个县级地点。四种物种,绿头鸭(Anas platyrhynchos)(2,848例,16.66%)、加拿大雁(Branta canadensis)(1,496例,8.75%)、绿翅鸭(Anas carolinensis)(1,364例,7.98%)和雪雁(Anser caerulescens)(1,084例,6.34%),共占检测总数的39.73%。在商业家禽中,分别报告了532例高致病性禽流感H5在火鸡养殖场、148例在蛋鸡养殖场、99例在肉鸡养殖场和89例在商业鸭养殖场。多个跨物种溢出事件呈现出从东向西扩展的趋势。2022年初,绿头鸭的检测先于东北部蛋鸡和鸭子养殖场的暴发事件,而2022年夏季在太平洋和落基山脉西部,加拿大雁的检测与火鸡养殖场的暴发事件重叠。2025年中西部地区出现疫情回升,雪雁和加拿大雁的检测与火鸡和蛋鸡群中的严重暴发事件重叠。此外,在2025年秋季至冬季的上中西部地区,加拿大雁和绿头鸭的检测与商业鸭养殖场的暴发事件重叠。研究结果表明,在野生水禽与家禽交界处,高致病性禽流感H5呈现出基于媒介的特定时空动态。农场生物安全策略必须适应这些反复出现的、基于媒介的特定风险。

问题与背景
2022年2月起,北美地区出现的高致病性禽流感(高致病性禽流感)H5N1 2.3.4.4b分支凸显了感染风险的动态性、不可预测性和区域性差异。
设计与对象
分析了2022年1月至2026年1月期间,北美地区在野生鸟类和商业家禽中公开的高致病性禽流感H5检测数据,按县进行分析。
核心方法
采用回顾性时空置换模型来识别和扫描检测率高于预期的聚集区域。
主要结果
在野生鸟类中,共报告了17,091例高致病性禽流感H5检测,涉及1,467个县级地点。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,在野生水禽与家禽交界处,高致病性禽流感H5呈现出基于媒介的特定时空动态。
科研与公卫意义
农场生物安全策略必须适应这些反复出现的、基于媒介的特定风险。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据未报告具体研究设计的局限性。

Spatiotemporal clustering of highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) H5N1 at the wild waterfowl-poultry interface: Vector-specific spillover risks in the U.S., 2022–2025

Authors: Csaba Varga
Original abstract (English)
The emergence of the highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b in North America, beginning in February 2022, has highlighted the dynamic, unpredictable, and regionally variable risk of infections. Studies are needed to assess the spatiotemporal clustering of HPAI H5 at the interface between wild waterfowl and commercial poultry to better understand and mitigate this risk. Publicly available data on HPAI H5 detections in wild birds and commercial poultry from January 2022 to January 2026 were analyzed at the county level. Retrospective space-time permutation models were used to identify and scan for clusters with higher-than-expected detection rates. A total of 17,091 HPAI H5 detections were reported in wild birds across 1,467 county-level locations. Four species, Mallard ( Anas platyrhynchos ) (2,848 detections, 16.66%), Canada goose ( Branta canadensis ) (1,496, 8.75%), Green-winged teal ( Anas carolinensis ) (1,364, 7.98%), and Snow goose ( Anser caerulescens ) (1,084, 6.34%), accounted for 39.73% of detections. In commercial poultry, 532 outbreaks in turkey operations, 148 outbreaks in table-egg layer operations, 99 outbreaks in broiler chicken operations, and 89 outbreaks in commercial duck operations were reported, respectively. Several spillover events followed an east-to-west expansion. In early 2022, mallard detections preceded outbreaks in Northeast egg-layer and duck farms, while snow goose detections in the Upper Midwest coincided with turkey farm outbreaks. In the Pacific and Mountain West during summer 2022, detections in Canada geese overlapped with turkey farm outbreaks. A resurgence occurred in the Midwest (2025), with snow and Canada goose detections overlapping severe outbreaks in turkey and layer flocks. Additionally, in the Upper Midwest, Canada goose and mallard detections overlapped with outbreaks in commercial duck farms during fall-winter 2025. The study findings demonstrate distinct vector-based transmission dynamics of HPAI H5 at the wild waterfowl-poultry interface. Farm biosecurity strategies must adapt to these recurrent, vector-specific risks.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The emergence of the highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b in North America has highlighted the dynamic, unpredictable, and regionally variable risk of infections.
Design and population
Publicly available data on HPAI H5 detections in wild birds and commercial poultry from January 2022 to January 2026 were analyzed at the county level.
Core methods
Retrospective space-time permutation models were used to identify and scan for clusters with higher-than-expected detection rates.
Main results
A total of 17,091 HPAI H5 detections were reported in wild birds across 1,467 county-level locations.
Interpretation and novelty
The study findings demonstrate distinct vector-based transmission dynamics of HPAI H5 at the wild waterfowl-poultry interface.
Scientific and public-health significance
Farm biosecurity strategies must adapt to these recurrent, vector-specific risks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations to the study design.

在小鼠和雪貂中,季节性流感疫苗接种在A(H5N1)挑战前对A(H5)疫苗接种的有限附加益处。

Limited added benefit of seasonal influenza vaccination before A(H5) vaccination in mice and ferrets challenged with A(H5N1).
作者： Masato Hatta, Ying Huang, Jeremy A. Duke, Joseph R. Rouse, Chen-Chen Feng, Xudong Lin, Li Wang, Melvyn R. Almanzar-Jordan, Zhu-Nan Li, James Stevens
摘要中文翻译在潜在A(H5N1)大流行早期,预计A(H5)特异性疫苗供应有限,这引发了这样一个问题:是否可以在A(H5)疫苗接种接种前使用已获批的季节性流感疫苗来增强保护作用。我们使用2.3.4.4b系A(H5N1)病毒,在小鼠和雪貂模型中评估了这一策略。季节性流感疫苗接种诱导了针对季节性血凝素的抗体,但并未诱导可检测的针对A(H5)的抗体,且在A(H5)疫苗接种接种后并未一致增强A(H5)特异性抗体反应。在致命挑战研究中,与单独接种A(H5)疫苗接种相比,季节性疫苗在A(H5)疫苗接种接种前的接种与一项小鼠实验中改善的结局相关,但在另外两项小鼠实验或雪貂中未观察到这种效果。这些发现表明,在所测试的条件下,季节性流感疫苗接种对A(H5)疫苗介导的A(H5N1)保护作用的附加益处有限。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
在潜在A(H5N1)大流行早期,预计A(H5)特异性疫苗供应有限,这引发了这样一个问题:是否可以在A(H5)疫苗接种接种前使用已获批的季节性流感疫苗来增强保护作用。
设计与对象
我们使用2.3.4.4b系A(H5N1)病毒,在小鼠和雪貂模型中评估了这一策略。
核心方法
季节性流感疫苗接种诱导了针对季节性血凝素的抗体,但并未诱导可检测的针对A(H5)的抗体,且在A(H5)疫苗接种接种后并未一致增强A(H5)特异性抗体反应。
主要结果
在致命挑战研究中,与单独接种A(H5)疫苗接种相比,季节性疫苗在A(H5)疫苗接种接种前的接种与一项小鼠实验中改善的结局相关,但在另外两项小鼠实验或雪貂中未观察到这种效果。
解释与创新
这些发现表明,在所测试的条件下,季节性流感疫苗接种对A(H5)疫苗介导的A(H5N1)保护作用的附加益处有限。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现谨慎地表明,季节性流感疫苗作为在A(H5)疫苗接种接种前增强对A(H5N1)保护的策略,在动物模型中的潜在效用有限。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告这些发现对人类人群的可推广性,也未报告季节性疫苗接种对A(H5N1)免疫力的长期影响。证据强度为中等。

Limited added benefit of seasonal influenza vaccination before A(H5) vaccination in mice and ferrets challenged with A(H5N1).

Authors: Masato Hatta, Ying Huang, Jeremy A. Duke, Joseph R. Rouse, Chen-Chen Feng, Xudong Lin, Li Wang, Melvyn R. Almanzar-Jordan, Zhu-Nan Li, James Stevens
Original abstract (English)
Limited A(H5)-specific vaccine supply is expected early in a potential A(H5N1) pandemic, raising the question of whether licensed seasonal influenza vaccines could enhance protection when administered before A(H5) vaccination. We evaluated this strategy in mouse and ferret models using clade 2.3.4.4b A(H5N1) viruses. Seasonal influenza vaccination induced antibodies to seasonal haemagglutinins but did not induce detectable antibodies against A(H5) and did not consistently enhance A(H5)-directed antibody responses after A(H5) vaccination. In lethal challenge studies, seasonal vaccine priming before A(H5) vaccination was associated with improved outcomes compared with A(H5) vaccination alone in one of three mouse experiments, but this effect was not observed in the other two mouse experiments or in ferrets. These findings suggest that seasonal influenza vaccine priming provides limited added benefit to A(H5) vaccine-mediated protection against A(H5N1) under the conditions tested.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Limited A(H5)-specific vaccine supply is expected early in a potential A(H5N1) pandemic, raising the question of whether licensed seasonal influenza vaccines could enhance protection when administered before A(H5) vaccination.
Design and population
We evaluated this strategy in mouse and ferret models using clade 2.3.4.4b A(H5N1) viruses.
Core methods
Seasonal influenza vaccination induced antibodies to seasonal haemagglutinins but did not induce detectable antibodies against A(H5) and did not consistently enhance A(H5)-directed antibody responses after A(H5) vaccination.
Main results
In lethal challenge studies, seasonal vaccine priming before A(H5) vaccination was associated with improved outcomes compared with A(H5) vaccination alone in one of three mouse experiments, but this effect was not observed in the other two mouse experiments or in ferrets.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings suggest that seasonal influenza vaccine priming provides limited added benefit to A(H5) vaccine-mediated protection against A(H5N1) under the conditions tested.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings cautiously inform the potential limited utility of seasonal influenza vaccines as a pre-vaccination strategy for enhancing protection against A(H5N1) in animal models.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report on the generalizability of the findings to human populations or the long-term effects of seasonal vaccine priming on A(H5N1) immunity. The evidence strength is moderate.

基于CRISPR的简化检测和亚型鉴定H5和H7 禽流感方法

Streamlined CRISPR-based assays for detection and subtyping of H5 and H7 avian influenza
作者： Yu-Jia Huang, Andrew Guo, Gordon Adams, Jacob E. Lemieux, Cameron Myhrvold, Yujia Huang
摘要中文翻译禽流感病毒(AIVs)是人兽共患病病原体,由于其可能造成农业领域重大经济损失以及人类间传播引发大流行的潜在风险,对全球构成日益增长的威胁。鉴于此,我们需要快速、敏感且特异的检测方法来识别和区分流行的AIVs。目前的AIV诊断方法需要专门的实验室设备,限制了其在野外和资源匮乏地区的应用。在此,我们开发了三种基于CRISPR的简化感染监测与流行病防控(SHINE)检测方法,以解决这些限制。我们设计、优化并验证了针对H5 AIV的SHINE检测方法,在测试季节性流感阳性临床样本时实现了100%的特异性,并达到121.7 copies/μL的检测限。我们还开发了针对2.3.4.4b A(H5N1)和欧亚H7谱系的SHINE检测方法。这些SHINE检测方法为AIV 诊断和监测提供了一个有前景的平台,特别是在非实验室环境中。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在应对禽流感病毒(AIVs)日益增长的全球威胁,因为其可能造成农业领域重大经济损失以及人类间传播引发大流行的潜在风险。研究目标是开发快速、敏感且特异的检测方法,用于检测和区分流行的AIVs。
设计与对象
本研究涉及开发三种针对H5和H7 AIV谱系的基于CRISPR的SHINE检测方法。这些检测方法使用临床样本和特定的AIV谱系进行设计、优化和验证。
核心方法
方法包括设计、优化和验证针对H5 AIV的基于CRISPR的SHINE检测方法,实现了100%的特异性和121.7 copies/μL的检测限。此外,还开发了针对2.3.4.4b A(H5N1)和欧亚H7谱系的SHINE检测方法。
主要结果
针对H5 AIV的SHINE检测方法实现了100%的特异性和121.7 copies/μL的检测限。针对2.3.4.4b A(H5N1)和欧亚H7谱系的SHINE检测方法也得到了开发。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为为AIV 诊断和监测提供了一个有前景的平台,特别是在非实验室环境中。这些SHINE检测方法的新颖性在于其能够克服当前AIV诊断方法的局限性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为开发AIVs的快速诊断工具提供信息,特别是在野外和资源匮乏地区,有助于增强监测和控制措施。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性包括需要在多样化的临床环境中进一步验证,以及样本可能存在偏差。由于验证的初步性质,证据强度中等。

Streamlined CRISPR-based assays for detection and subtyping of H5 and H7 avian influenza

Authors: Yu-Jia Huang, Andrew Guo, Gordon Adams, Jacob E. Lemieux, Cameron Myhrvold, Yujia Huang
Original abstract (English)
Avian influenza viruses (AIVs) are zoonotic pathogens that pose an increasing global threat due to their potential for significant economic losses in agriculture, and the risk of a pandemic should human-to-human transmission occur. In light of this, we need rapid, sensitive, and specific tests to detect and differentiate circulating AIVs. Current AIV diagnostic methods require specialized laboratory equipment, limiting their use in the field and in low-resource settings. Here, we develop three CRISPR-based Streamlined Highlighting of Infections to Navigate Epidemics (SHINE) assays that address these limitations. We designed, optimized, and validated a SHINE assay for H5 AIV, achieving 100% specificity when tested on seasonal-influenza-positive clinical samples, and a limit of detection of 121.7 copies/μL. We also developed SHINE assays targeting the 2.3.4.4b A(H5N1) and Eurasian H7 lineages. Together, these SHINE assays offer a promising platform for AIV diagnosis and surveillance, particularly outside laboratory settings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study was needed to address the increasing global threat of avian influenza viruses (AIVs) due to their potential for significant economic losses in agriculture and the risk of a pandemic. The research aimed to develop rapid, sensitive, and specific tests for detecting and differentiating circulating AIVs.
Design and population
The study involved developing three CRISPR-based SHINE assays targeting H5 and H7 AIV lineages. The assays were designed, optimized, and validated using clinical samples and specific AIV lineages.
Core methods
The methods included designing, optimizing, and validating CRISPR-based SHINE assays for H5 AIV, achieving 100% specificity and a limit of detection of 121.7 copies/μL. Additional SHINE assays were developed for 2.3.4.4b A(H5N1) and Eurasian H7 lineages.
Main results
The SHINE assays for H5 AIV achieved 100% specificity and a limit of detection of 121.7 copies/μL. SHINE assays for 2.3.4.4b A(H5N1) and Eurasian H7 lineages were also developed.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as offering a promising platform for AIV diagnosis and surveillance, particularly outside laboratory settings. The SHINE assays are novel in their ability to address the limitations of current AIV diagnostic methods.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings could inform the development of rapid diagnostic tools for AIVs, particularly in field and low-resource settings, enhancing surveillance and control efforts.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the need for further validation in diverse clinical settings and the potential for sample bias. The evidence strength is moderate due to the preliminary nature of the validation.

2025-2026冬季韩国检测到的Clade 2.3.4.4b H5Nx高致病性禽流感病毒的遗传多样性及H5N1和H5N9病毒的致病性

Genetic Diversity of Clade 2.3.4.4b H5Nx High Pathogenicity Avian Influenza Viruses Detected in Korea During the 2025-2026 Winter Season and Pathogenicity of H5N1 and H5N9 Viruses.
作者： Yunyueng Jang, Ra Mi Cha, Min-Ji Park, Jong-Min Kim, Eui Hyeon Lim, Gyeong-Beom Heo, Se-Hee An, Bina Lee, Hyun-Ji Seo, Kwang-Nyeong Lee
摘要中文翻译2025-2026冬季在韩国的家禽和野生鸟类中检测到Clade 2.3.4.4b H5N1、H5N6和H5N9高致病性禽流感病毒(HPAIVs)。在此期间,韩国家禽中发生了62起H5Nx HPAI 暴发,野生鸟类中报告了63例病例。基因组星座分析揭示了显著的基因型多样性。大多数H5N1和H5N9基因型是重组病毒,包含自2022年以来东亚Clade 2.3.4.4b H5N1 HPAIVs的基因片段以及来自野生鸟类的低致病性禽流感病毒的基因片段。值得注意的是,在2025年快速传播于欧洲的欧洲H5N1基因型EA-2024-DI.2.1及其重组病毒在韩国被发现。此外,在野生鸟类中还检测到了新的家禽基因型,支持了野生鸟类向家禽的跨物种溢出,而之前季节检测到的一个基因型则在小规模家禽养殖场中被发现。在鸡中,主要的25G2基因型H5N1(C588)和N9-G2基因型H5N9(C722)分别表现出103.3和103.2 EID50的中位致死剂量(LD50)值,显示出高感染力。在幼鸭中,H5N1导致了快速的死亡,而H5N9则仅引起有限的临床症状,没有死亡。两种病毒均能传播给所有接触的鸭子,增加了对家禽的传播风险。尽管加强了监测和早期检测以限制病毒传播,但在2025-2026冬季仍发生了H5Nx HPAIV 暴发。这些发现强调了持续进行监测的重要性,以及对新引入的H5Nx HPAIVs进行快速基因组和致病性特征分析的必要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查2025-2026冬季韩国检测到的Clade 2.3.4.4b H5Nx高致病性禽流感病毒的遗传多样性,并评估其致病性。
设计与对象
本研究分析了2025-2026冬季韩国家禽和野生鸟类中H5Nx高致病性禽流感(HPAIV)病毒,报告了62起家禽中的暴发和63例野生鸟类中的病例。
核心方法
使用基因组星座分析来评估病毒的遗传多样性,并通过感染性测试在鸡和幼鸭中评估其致病性。
主要结果
在韩国的家禽和野生鸟类中检测到了Clade 2.3.4.4b H5N1、H5N6和H5N9 HPAIVs,其中大多数基因型是重组病毒,包含自2022年以来东亚Clade 2.3.4.4b H5N1 HPAIVs的基因片段以及来自野生鸟类的低致病性禽流感病毒的基因片段。
解释与创新
在野生鸟类中检测到新的家禽基因型,支持了野生鸟类向家禽的跨物种溢出可能性,而之前季节检测到的一个基因型在小规模家禽养殖场中被发现,表明存在持续传播的潜在风险。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调了持续进行监测的重要性,以及对新引入的H5Nx HPAIVs进行快速基因组和致病性特征分析的必要性,以减轻其在家禽和公共卫生方面的传播和影响。
局限与证据强度
本研究的结果基于暴发数据和感染性测试,可能无法完全反映病毒传播动态的复杂性,且由于研究的观察性质,证据强度中等。

Genetic Diversity of Clade 2.3.4.4b H5Nx High Pathogenicity Avian Influenza Viruses Detected in Korea During the 2025-2026 Winter Season and Pathogenicity of H5N1 and H5N9 Viruses.

Authors: Yunyueng Jang, Ra Mi Cha, Min-Ji Park, Jong-Min Kim, Eui Hyeon Lim, Gyeong-Beom Heo, Se-Hee An, Bina Lee, Hyun-Ji Seo, Kwang-Nyeong Lee
Original abstract (English)
Clade 2.3.4.4b H5N1, H5N6, and H5N9 high pathogenicity avian influenza viruses (HPAIVs) were detected in poultry and wild birds during the 2025-2026 winter season in Korea. During this period, 62 H5Nx HPAI outbreaks occurred in poultry, and 63 cases were reported in wild birds. Genome constellation analysis revealed substantial genotype diversity. Most H5N1 and H5N9 genotypes were reassortants containing gene segments derived from East Asian clade 2.3.4.4b H5N1 HPAIVs since 2022 and low-pathogenicity avian influenza viruses from wild birds. Notably, the European H5N1 genotype EA-2024-DI.2.1, which spread rapidly across Europe in 2025, and its reassortants were identified in Korea. The novel poultry genotypes were also detected in wild birds, supporting wild bird to poultry spillover, whereas one genotype detected in previous season was identified in small-scale poultry farms. H5N1 (C588) of major 25G2 genotype in poultry and H5N9 (C722) of N9-G2 genotype showed high infectivity in chickens with 103.3 and 103.2 EID50 of median lethal dose (LD50) values, respectively. The predominance of the 25G2 genotype in poultry may have been influenced by efficient infection and virus introduction into high-density poultry production areas, potentially facilitating horizontal transmission under field conditions. In young ducks, H5N1 caused rapid mortality, whereas H5N9 induced limited clinical signs without mortality. Both viruses were transmitted to all contact ducks, increasing the risk of transmission to poultry. Despite intensified surveillance and early detection efforts to limit virus spread, H5Nx HPAIV outbreaks occurred during the 2025-2026 winter season. These findings highlight the need for continued surveillance, together with rapid genomic and pathogenic characterization of newly introduced viruses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the genetic diversity of clade 2.3.4.4b H5Nx high pathogenicity avian influenza viruses in Korea during the 2025-2026 winter season and assess their pathogenicity.
Design and population
The study analyzed H5Nx high pathogenicity avian influenza (HPAIV) outbreaks in poultry and wild birds in Korea during the 2025-2026 winter season, reporting 62 outbreaks in poultry and 63 cases in wild birds.
Core methods
Genome constellation analysis was used to assess the genetic diversity of the viruses, and pathogenicity was evaluated in chickens and young ducks through infectivity testing.
Main results
Clade 2.3.4.4b H5N1, H5N6, and H5N9 HPAIVs were detected in poultry and wild birds, with most genotypes being reassortants containing gene segments from East Asian clade 2.3.4.4b H5N1 HPAIVs since 2022 and low-pathogenicity avian influenza viruses from wild birds.
Interpretation and novelty
The detection of novel poultry genotypes in wild birds supports the possibility of wild bird to poultry spillover, and the presence of a previous season genotype in small-scale poultry farms indicates potential for sustained transmission.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings emphasize the importance of continued surveillance and rapid genomic and pathogenic characterization of newly introduced H5Nx HPAIVs to mitigate their spread and impact on poultry and public health.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's results are based on outbreak data and infectivity testing, which may not fully capture the complexity of viral transmission dynamics, and the evidence is moderate due to the observational nature of the study.

北极狐中广泛暴露于H5 禽流感病毒的血清学证据:挪威斯瓦尔巴群岛

Serological Evidence of Widespread Exposure to H5 Avian Influenza Virus in Arctic Foxes in Svalbard, Norway
作者： Johanna Hol Fosse, Eva Fuglei, Francesco Bonfante, Line Olsen, Luca Bordes, Rebecca K. Davidson, Mork T, Ida Kristin Myhrvold, Lone Thiel Engerdahl, Sandra Venema
摘要中文翻译摘要 高致病性禽流感(HPAIV)H5 clade 2.3.4.4b 高致病性禽流感病毒的持续传播在全球范围内导致了死亡率种群的广泛感染,并且对哺乳动物的传播风险正在增加。2022年,H5 HPAIV在挪威斯瓦尔巴群岛首次出现,并随后在野生鸟类、海象、北极熊和北极狐中被检测到。为了了解斯瓦尔巴群岛北极狐种群的暴露情况,我们分析了2006-2015年(n = 56)、2023-2024年(n = 112)和2024-2025年(n = 94)捕获的尸体的体液,检测针对H5 禽流感病毒(anti-H5)、流感A型核蛋白(anti-NP)和神经氨酸酶亚型的抗体,使用ELISA、血凝抑制(HI)试验和多重检测方法。仅2006-2015年的三个样本对anti-H5呈阳性,被解释为假阳性。在2023-2024年,anti-H5的血清阳性率很高(95%),并由较低的anti-NP血清阳性率(85%)和与混合H5N1(42%)和H5N5(52%)暴露一致的抗体谱所支持。在2024-2025年,anti-H5和anti-NP的血清阳性率分别保持在83%和57%,其中H5N5(87%)的暴露显著高于H5N1(3%)。一部分anti-H5阳性样本通过HI试验呈阳性(2023-2024年:30%;2024-2025年:14%)。2023-2024年,仅限于上一季节暴露的幼年个体anti-H5血清阳性率的比值(OR)高于2024-2025年(OR 5.5)。对63名个体的肺部提取物进行配对分析,使用一种针对食肉动物改良的间接anti-H5 ELISA,结果与体液检测结果一致。我们的研究结果表明,北极狐中广泛存在H5病毒暴露。结合偶尔报告的H5 HPAIV进展为致命性病例,这凸显了持续监测北极狐种群以评估其生态影响的必要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查北极狐对H5 禽流感病毒的种群水平暴露情况,鉴于H5 clade 2.3.4.4b 高致病性禽流感病毒(HPAIV)在全球范围的传播及其对哺乳动物传播风险的增加。
设计与对象
本研究分析了三个时间段捕获的尸体的体液:2006-2015年(n = 56)、2023-2024年(n = 112)和2024-2025年(n = 94),重点关注2022年H5 HPAIV首次出现在挪威斯瓦尔巴群岛的北极狐种群。
核心方法
使用ELISA、血凝抑制(HI)试验和多重检测方法,检测针对H5 禽流感病毒(anti-H5)、流感A型核蛋白(anti-NP)和神经氨酸酶亚型的抗体。还对63名个体的肺部提取物进行配对分析,使用一种针对食肉动物改良的间接anti-H5 ELISA。
主要结果
在2023-2024年,95%的样本对anti-H5呈阳性,其中42%显示暴露于H5N1,52%显示暴露于H5N5。在2024-2025年,anti-H5和anti-NP的血清阳性率分别保持在83%和57%,其中H5N5的暴露显著高于H5N1(87% vs. 3%)。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,北极狐中广泛存在H5病毒暴露,随着时间推移,主导亚型从H5N1转变为H5N5,这表明病毒动态和宿主相互作用可能发生了变化。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果强调了监测北极狐种群中H5 禽流感病毒的重要性,以更好地理解其生态影响以及其对其他物种(包括人类)传播的潜力。
局限与证据强度
由于早期样本可能存在假阳性,且依赖血清学数据而没有直接病毒检测,本研究的证据存在一定的局限性,导致研究结果的可信度中等。

Serological Evidence of Widespread Exposure to H5 Avian Influenza Virus in Arctic Foxes in Svalbard, Norway

Authors: Johanna Hol Fosse, Eva Fuglei, Francesco Bonfante, Line Olsen, Luca Bordes, Rebecca K. Davidson, Mork T, Ida Kristin Myhrvold, Lone Thiel Engerdahl, Sandra Venema
Original abstract (English)
Abstract The continued circulation of H5 clade 2.3.4.4b highly pathogenic avian influenza virus (HPAIV) has caused extensive mortality in wild bird populations worldwide with increasing spillover to mammals. In 2022, H5 HPAIV emerged in Svalbard, Norway, with subsequent detections in wild birds, walruses, polar bears, and arctic foxes. To understand the population-level exposure among Svalbard arctic foxes, we analysed body fluids from carcasses trapped in 2006-2015 (n = 56), 2023-2024 (n = 112), and 2024-2025 (n = 94) for antibodies to H5 avian influenza (anti- H5), influenza A nucleoprotein (anti-NP), and neuraminidase subtypes using ELISAs, haemagglutination inhibition (HI), and a multiplex assay. Only three samples from 2006-2015 tested positive for anti-H5 and were interpreted as false positives. In 2023-2024, seropositivity for anti-H5 was high (95%), supported by a lower anti-NP seropositivity (85%) and antibody profiles consistent with mixed H5N1 (42%) and H5N5 (52%) exposure. In 2024-2025, anti-H5 and anti-NP seroprevalences remained high (83% and 57%), with H5N5 (87%) exposure predominating over H5N1 (3%). A subset of anti-H5-positive samples tested positive by HI (2023-2024: 30%; 2024-2025: 14%). Juveniles with exposure limited to the previous season had higher odds of anti-H5 seropositivity in 2023-2024 than in 2024-2025 (OR 5.5). Analysis of paired lung extracts from a subset of individuals (n = 63) yielded results concordant with body fluids, using an indirect anti-H5 ELISA adapted for carnivores. Our findings demonstrate widespread H5 virus exposure. Together with occasional reports of progression to fatal HPAI, this highlights the need for continued population monitoring to evaluate ecological consequences.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the population-level exposure of arctic foxes to H5 avian influenza virus, given the global spread of H5 clade 2.3.4.4b highly pathogenic avian influenza virus (HPAIV) and its increasing spillover to mammals.
Design and population
The study analyzed body fluids from carcasses trapped in three time periods: 2006-2015 (n = 56), 2023-2024 (n = 112), and 2024-2025 (n = 94), focusing on arctic foxes in Svalbard, Norway, where H5 HPAIV emerged in 2022.
Core methods
Antibodies to H5 avian influenza (anti-H5), influenza A nucleoprotein (anti-NP), and neuraminidase subtypes were measured using ELISAs, haemagglutination inhibition (HI), and a multiplex assay. Paired lung extracts from a subset of individuals (n = 63) were also analyzed using an indirect anti-H5 ELISA adapted for carnivores.
Main results
In 2023-2024, 95% of samples tested positive for anti-H5, with 42% showing exposure to H5N1 and 52% to H5N5. In 2024-2025, anti-H5 and anti-NP seroprevalences remained high at 83% and 57%, respectively, with H5N5 exposure predominating (87%) over H5N1 (3%).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate widespread H5 virus exposure in arctic foxes, with a shift in dominant subtype from H5N1 to H5N5 over time, indicating potential changes in viral dynamics and host interactions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results highlight the importance of monitoring arctic fox populations for H5 avian influenza virus to better understand its ecological impact and potential for spillover to other species, including humans.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence is limited by the potential for false positives in earlier samples and the reliance on serological data without direct viral detection, resulting in moderate confidence in the findings.

DEAD-box RNA解旋酶10与流感病毒PB1聚合酶的相互作用特异性调控流感A病毒复制。

Interaction between DEAD-box RNA helicase 10 and influenza PB1 polymerase selectively regulates influenza A virus replication.
作者： Rizwan Ullah, Wei Chen, Ling-Kai Zhang, Chao-Xuan Guo, Bin Ren, Xin-Yi Pei, Dong Yang, Qing-Shuai Sun, Xin-Yu Gao, Hong-Bo Zhou
摘要中文翻译未标注:识别介导禽流感病毒在哺乳动物中适应的宿主因子对于监测人兽共患病潜力至关重要。尽管已知病毒聚合酶的适应性会影响跨物种溢出,但特定宿主因子与不同病毒聚合酶的相互作用仍需进一步探索。本研究考察了人类DEAD-box RNA解旋酶(DDXs)作为流感聚合酶活性的潜在调控因子。对16种DDXs的筛选发现,DDX10能够特异性增强人类适应型H1N1病毒(包括2009年大流行株)的聚合酶活性和复制,但对禽源H9N2病毒则无此作用。这种差异性活性与DDX10与人类来源PB1(Cal04/H1N1)相比,与禽源PB1(BJ16/H9N2)显示出更强的相互作用有关。这种株特异性由PB1催化结构域内的两个残基(336和364)介导:将H1N1型残基(I336和I364)引入H9N2-PB1中可使其对DDX10产生响应,而将H1N1-PB1中的残基替换为H9N2型则会消除这种响应。本研究将DDX10定义为一种潜在的宿主因子,其特异性支持流感聚合酶活性,揭示了特定分子界面,有助于理解不同病毒亚型在人类细胞中复制效率的机制,并提供了关于宿主适应机制的见解。重要性:本研究识别了DDX10作为一种株特异性的宿主因子,其特异性调控流感A病毒复制。通过展示DDX10如何通过直接与病毒PB1蛋白相互作用,特异性增强人类适应型H1N1病毒而非禽源H9N2病毒的复制能力,并通过定位PB1中的两个关键残基(位置336和364)来调控这种选择性,我们的研究为理解单一宿主因子如何调节病毒适应性和病毒适应性提供了机制框架。这些发现对于预测跨物种溢出潜力具有重要意义,并可能指导针对宿主的靶向策略的开发。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
识别介导禽流感病毒在哺乳动物中适应的宿主因子对于监测人兽共患病潜力至关重要。尽管已知病毒聚合酶的适应性会影响跨物种溢出,但特定宿主因子与不同病毒聚合酶的相互作用仍需进一步探索。
设计与对象
本研究考察了人类DEAD-box RNA解旋酶(DDXs)作为流感聚合酶活性的潜在调控因子。研究涉及对16种DDXs的筛选,并聚焦于人类适应型H1N1病毒(包括2009年大流行株)和禽源H9N2病毒。
核心方法
本研究通过筛选16种DDXs以识别流感聚合酶活性的潜在调控因子。分析了DDX10与病毒PB1蛋白的相互作用,并定位了PB1中的关键残基(位置336和364)。
主要结果
DDX10特异性增强了人类适应型H1N1病毒(包括2009年大流行株)的聚合酶活性和复制,但对禽源H9N2病毒则无此作用。这种差异性活性与DDX10与人类来源PB1(Cal04/H1N1)相比,与禽源PB1(BJ16/H9N2)显示出更强的相互作用有关。
解释与创新
本研究将DDX10定义为一种潜在的宿主因子,其特异性调控流感A病毒复制。通过展示DDX10如何通过直接与病毒PB1蛋白相互作用,特异性增强人类适应型H1N1病毒而非禽源H9N2病毒的复制能力,并通过定位PB1中的两个关键残基(位置336和364)来调控这种选择性,我们的研究为理解单一宿主因子如何调节病毒适应性和病毒适应性提供了机制框架。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现对于预测跨物种溢出潜力具有重要意义,并可能指导针对宿主的靶向策略的开发。
局限与证据强度
本研究将DDX10定义为一种潜在的宿主因子,其特异性支持流感聚合酶活性,揭示了特定分子界面,有助于理解不同病毒亚型在人类细胞中复制效率的机制,并提供了关于宿主适应机制的见解。然而,本研究并未探讨这些发现是否适用于其他宿主或病毒亚型。

Interaction between DEAD-box RNA helicase 10 and influenza PB1 polymerase selectively regulates influenza A virus replication.

Authors: Rizwan Ullah, Wei Chen, Ling-Kai Zhang, Chao-Xuan Guo, Bin Ren, Xin-Yi Pei, Dong Yang, Qing-Shuai Sun, Xin-Yu Gao, Hong-Bo Zhou
Original abstract (English)
UNLABELLED: Identifying the host factors that mediate avian influenza virus adaptation in mammals is important for monitoring zoonotic potential. Although viral polymerase adaptations are known to influence cross-species transmission, the engagement of specific host factors with divergent viral polymerases remains to be explored. We examined human DEAD-box RNA helicases (DDXs) as potential regulators of influenza polymerase activity. A screen of 16 DDXs identified DDX10 as a factor that selectively enhanced the polymerase activity and replication of human-adapted H1N1 viruses, including the 2009 pandemic strain, but not avian-origin H9N2 viruses. This differential activity was associated with DDX10 showing stronger interaction with human-origin PB1 (Cal04/H1N1) than with avian-origin PB1 (BJ16/H9N2). Strain specificity was mediated by two residues (336 and 364) within the PB1 catalytic domain: introducing H1N1-type residues (I336 and I364) into H9N2-PB1 conferred DDX10 responsiveness, while reciprocal mutations in H1N1-PB1 abolished it. This work defines DDX10 as a potential host factor that differentially supports influenza polymerase activity, revealing a specific molecular interface that contributes to the replication efficiency of distinct viral subtypes in human cells and providing insight into host-adaptive mechanisms. IMPORTANCE: This study identifies DDX10 as a strain-specific host factor that differentially regulates influenza A virus replication. By demonstrating that DDX10 selectively enhances human-adapted H1N1 viruses over avian-origin H9N2 viruses through direct interaction with the viral PB1 protein, and by mapping two critical residues (positions 336 and 364) in PB1 that govern this selectivity, our work provides a mechanistic framework for understanding how a single host factor can modulate viral fitness and host adaptation. These findings have important implications for predicting cross-species transmission potential and may guide the development of host-targeted antiviral strategies.

Question and background
Identifying host factors that mediate avian influenza virus adaptation in mammals is important for monitoring zoonotic potential. Although viral polymerase adaptations are known to influence cross-species transmission, the engagement of specific host factors with divergent viral polymerases remains to be explored.
Design and population
The study examined human DEAD-box RNA helicases (DDXs) as potential regulators of influenza polymerase activity. The research involved a screen of 16 DDXs and focused on human-adapted H1N1 viruses, including the 2009 pandemic strain, and avian-origin H9N2 viruses.
Core methods
The study involved screening 16 DDXs to identify potential regulators of influenza polymerase activity. The interaction between DDX10 and the viral PB1 protein was analyzed, and critical residues (positions 336 and 364) in PB1 were mapped.
Main results

This differential activity was associated with DDX10 showing stronger interaction with human-origin PB1 (Cal04/H1N1) than with avian-origin PB1 (BJ16/H9N2).
Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance
These findings have important implications for predicting cross-species transmission potential and may guide the development of host-targeted antiviral strategies.

This work defines DDX10 as a potential host factor that differentially supports influenza polymerase activity, revealing a specific molecular interface that contributes to the replication efficiency of distinct viral subtypes in human cells and providing insight into host-adaptive mechanisms. However, the study does not address the generalizability of these findings to other host or viral subtypes.

Evaluating U.S. Federal Zoonotic Records Against National One Health Goals: A Multi-LLM Assessment Across COVID-19, Monkeypox, and H5N1

Evaluating U.S. Federal Zoonotic Records Against National One Health Goals: A Multi-LLM Assessment Across COVID-19, Monkeypox, and H5N1
作者： Bowen Long, Fangya Tan, Jinghang Zhang
摘要中文翻译(1) 背景:人兽共患病需要在人类、动物和环境健康领域进行协调行动。尽管已有大量研究关注于暴发预测、预测和监测,但对系统评估One Health原则在政府政策文件中的体现却关注较少。(2) 方法:我们开发了一个多大语言模型(LLM)框架,用于评估477份与新冠、猴痘和高致病性禽流感(H5N1)相关的美国政府文件。使用涵盖美国国家One Health框架三个目标的19项评分标准,对机构技术指南和联邦公报文件进行了评估。Claude、GPT和Gemini独立评估了这些文件。评估了模型间的共识度和人工验证,并进行了敏感性分析,以检验不同的文档分块到文档的聚合阈值以及文档长度的潜在影响。(3) 结果:One Health原则的一致性总体有限,没有任何疾病×文档类型×目标组合超过39%。文档类型显示出最明显的一致性差异,机构技术指南在三个目标上的一致性比联邦公报文件高出约13–31个百分点,而不同疾病之间的一致性差异则不太一致。敏感性分析支持了在不同聚合阈值下以及限制类型B文档长度后的主要文档类型模式。在排除了一个对措辞敏感的评分标准项后,三个LLM在87.1%的文档-评分项评估中达成一致,人类与模型之间的共识度(Cohen’s κ = 0.513–0.692)与人类之间的共识度(κ = 0.649)相当。(4) 结论:本研究引入了一个专门构建的政策语料库和结构化的多LLM评估框架,用于探讨One Health原则在政府文件中的体现。最明显差异出现在文档类型而非疾病之间,且主要发现普遍稳定于不同的分析假设下。这些发现将人兽共患病研究从暴发预测和监测扩展到了对One Health政策一致性的系统评估。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估美国联邦人兽共患病记录与国家One Health目标的一致性,填补了系统评估这些原则在政策实施中体现的空白。
设计与对象
The study analyzed 477 U.S. government documents related to COVID-19, monkeypox, and highly pathogenic avian influenza (H5N1), focusing on how these documents reflect the three goals of the National One Health Framework.
核心方法
采用多大语言模型(LLM)框架对文件进行评估,依据19项评分标准。评估了模型间的共识度和人工验证,并进行了敏感性分析,以检验不同的聚合阈值和文档长度的影响。
主要结果
One Health原则的一致性总体有限,没有任何疾病×文档类型×目标组合超过39%。文档类型显示出最明显的一致性差异,机构技术指南在三个目标上的一致性比联邦公报文件高出约13–31个百分点。
解释与创新
本研究引入了一个专门构建的政策语料库和结构化的多LLM评估框架,用于探讨One Health原则在政府文件中的体现,强调了对政策一致性进行更系统评估的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明需要改进政策与One Health原则的一致性,特别是在人兽共患病管理方面,并提出了一个用于未来评估政府记录的框架。
局限与证据强度
研究结果受到文档长度和措辞敏感性的潜在影响,但敏感性分析支持了主要的文档类型模式,表明证据强度中等。

Evaluating U.S. Federal Zoonotic Records Against National One Health Goals: A Multi-LLM Assessment Across COVID-19, Monkeypox, and H5N1

Authors: Bowen Long, Fangya Tan, Jinghang Zhang
Original abstract (English)
(1) Background: Zoonotic diseases require coordinated action across human, animal, and environmental health sectors. While substantial research has focused on outbreak prediction, forecasting, and surveillance, less attention has been given to systematically evaluating how One Health principles are reflected in government policy documents. (2) Methods: We developed a multi-large language model (LLM) framework to evaluate 477 U.S. government documents covering COVID-19, monkeypox, and highly pathogenic avian influenza (H5N1). Agency technical guidance and Federal Register documents were evaluated using a 19-item rubric covering three goals of the U.S. National One Health Framework: coordination and communication, outbreak response, and surveillance. Claude, GPT, and Gemini independently evaluated the documents. Inter-model agreement and human validation were assessed, and sensitivity analyses examined alternative chunk-to-document aggregation thresholds and the potential influence of document length. (3) Results: One Health alignment was generally limited, with no disease × document-type × goal combination exceeding 39%. Document type showed the clearest differences, with agency technical guidance showing approximately 13–31 percentage-point higher alignment than Federal Register documents across the three goals, while differences across diseases were less consistent. Sensitivity analyses supported the main document-type pattern across alternative aggregation thresholds and after restricting Type B documents by length. After excluding one wording-sensitive rubric item, the three LLMs reached unanimous agreement in 87.1% of document-item evaluations, with human–model agreement (Cohen’s κ = 0.513–0.692) comparable to human–human agreement (κ = 0.649). (4) Conclusions: This study introduces a purpose-built policy corpus and structured multi-LLM evaluation framework for examining how One Health principles are reflected in government documents. The clearest differences were observed across document types rather than diseases, and the main findings were generally stable across alternative analytical assumptions. Together, these findings extend zoonotic-disease research beyond outbreak prediction and surveillance toward systematic evaluation of One Health policy alignment.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate how well U.S. federal zoonotic records align with the National One Health goals, addressing the gap in systematically assessing policy implementation of these principles.
Design and population
The study analyzed 477 U.S. government documents related to COVID-19, monkeypox, and highly pathogenic avian influenza (H5N1), focusing on how these documents reflect the three goals of the National One Health Framework.
Core methods
A multi-large language model (LLM) framework was used to evaluate the documents against a 19-item rubric. Inter-model agreement and human validation were assessed, and sensitivity analyses were conducted to examine alternative aggregation thresholds and document length effects.
Main results
One Health alignment was generally limited, with no disease × document-type × goal combination exceeding 39%. Document type showed the clearest differences, with agency technical guidance showing approximately 13–31 percentage-point higher alignment than Federal Register documents across the three goals.
Interpretation and novelty
The study introduces a purpose-built policy corpus and structured multi-LLM evaluation framework for examining One Health principles in government documents, highlighting the need for more systematic policy alignment assessments.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings inform the need for improved policy alignment with One Health principles, particularly in zoonotic disease management, and suggest a framework for future evaluations of government records.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's findings are limited by the potential influence of document length and wording sensitivity, but the main document-type pattern was supported by sensitivity analyses, indicating moderate evidence strength.

使用建模方法估计H5N1高致病性禽流感在奶牛群中的传播动力学

Estimation of the transmission dynamics of H5N1 HPAI outbreak in a dairy herd using a modeling approach
作者： Pranav Shrikant Kulkarni, Sharif S. Aly, Deniece R. R Williams, Wagdy R. ElAshmawy, Pranav S Pandit
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
2024年在美国19个州的奶牛群中出现的高致病性禽流感(HPAI 2.3.4.4b)引发了对潜在国家和全球人兽共患病影响的担忧。
设计与对象
本研究聚焦于加利福尼亚州的四家奶牛场,采用建模方法估计同群内HPAI 传播的流行病学参数。
核心方法
开发了一个经过验证的随机基于代理的模型,该模型结合了基于个体的SEIR 传播方法,并参数化了群内随机奶牛移动以模拟真实的奶牛管理实践。
主要结果
该模型估计了所有农场在观察到的暴发前15天内的平均R0约为10.7-10.8,每只易感牛与感染牛之间的接触平均有效传播率为4.5%。模拟结果显示,奶牛在围栏间的移动形成了局部的暴发簇,延长了整体暴发的持续时间。
解释与创新
结合分层基于代理的模型和贝叶斯近似,提供了关于HPAI在奶牛群内传播的流行病学的新见解,为未来模型开发和疾病控制策略提供了可操作的信息。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为HPAI 暴发在奶牛群中的监测和控制策略提供信息,有助于理解人兽共患病的传播动力学,并指导干预措施以减轻禽流感病毒的传播。
局限与证据强度
所有最近的建模工作都采用了同质的奶牛间传播模型,这些模型未能捕捉到动物混合、个体易感性和传染性差异以及不同围栏和泌乳群体中的临床发病率的真实异质性。由于模型假设的局限性,证据强度中等。

Estimation of the transmission dynamics of H5N1 HPAI outbreak in a dairy herd using a modeling approach

Authors: Pranav Shrikant Kulkarni, Sharif S. Aly, Deniece R. R Williams, Wagdy R. ElAshmawy, Pranav S Pandit
Original abstract (English)
The emergence of Highly Pathogenic Avian Influenza (HPAI 2.3.4.4b) in dairy herds in 2024 across 19 states in the United States of America has raised concerns regarding the potential national and global zoonotic impact. All recent modeling efforts implemented homogenous cattle-to-cattle (both intra and inter-herd) transmission models which did not capture the real-world heterogeneity in mixing of animals, individual variations in susceptibility and infectiousness and clinical incidences across pens and lactation groups. The aim of this study was to develop a heterogenous transmission model to estimate the epidemiological parameters for intra-herd HPAI transmission on Californian dairies. We developed a validated stochastic agent-based model to estimate the epidemiological parameters for intra-herd HPAI transmission on California dairies. The hierarchical agent-based model also parameterized stochastic cattle movements within-herd to simulate real dairy management practices. A cow-level SEIR transmission approach was assumed during the outbreak. A novel Bayesian Optimizer with Gaussian Process (BO-GP) was fitted to the agent-based model for validation which converged within 25-40 iterations (out of 100 per farm) with minimal loss over two distinct error metrics, namely, Poisson loss function and temporal distance metric. Our optimized simulations estimated an average R 0 was around 10.7-10.8 across all farms within the first 15 days of observed outbreak on four dairy farms with a mean effective transmission rate of 4.5% per contact between susceptible and infectious cows within each pen. Our model demonstrated that the movement of cows between pens ensured localized clusters of outbreaks within the sub-herds (pen population) that prolonged the overall outbreak within farms. We estimated the total duration of infection between 14.5 and 28 days, which is higher than the estimates from the homogenous models. With an integrated hierarchical agent-based model combined with Bayesian approximation, we produced actionable insights on the epidemiology of intra-farm spread of HPAI within cow herds, thereby guiding both future model development and applied disease control strategy.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The emergence of Highly Pathogenic Avian Influenza (HPAI 2.3.4.4b) in dairy herds in 2024 across 19 states in the United States of America has raised concerns regarding the potential national and global zoonotic impact.
Design and population
This study focused on four dairy farms in California to estimate the epidemiological parameters for intra-herd HPAI transmission using a modeling approach.
Core methods
A validated stochastic agent-based model was developed, incorporating a cow-level SEIR transmission approach and parameterizing stochastic cattle movements within-herd to simulate real dairy management practices.
Main results
The model estimated an average R0 of 10.7-10.8 across all farms within the first 15 days of the outbreak, with a mean effective transmission rate of 4.5% per contact between susceptible and infectious cows within each pen. The simulations showed that cow movements between pens created localized clusters of outbreaks, prolonging the overall outbreak duration.
Interpretation and novelty
The hierarchical agent-based model combined with Bayesian approximation provided novel insights into the epidemiology of intra-farm spread of HPAI within cow herds, offering actionable information for future model development and disease control strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform surveillance and control strategies for HPAI outbreaks in dairy herds, contributing to the understanding of zoonotic transmission dynamics and guiding interventions to mitigate the spread of avian influenza viruses.
Limitations and evidence strength
All recent modeling efforts implemented homogenous cattle-to-cattle transmission models which did not capture the real-world heterogeneity in animal mixing, individual variations in susceptibility and infectiousness, and clinical incidences across pens and lactation groups. The evidence is moderate due to the limitations of the model assumptions.

使用雪貂模型对首例人类病例中的 禽流感 A(H5N5) 病毒进行风险评估

Risk assessment of avian influenza A(H5N5) virus from the first human case using the ferret model
作者： Joanna Pulit-Penaloza, Jessica A. Belser, Nicole Brock, Troy J. Kieran, Claudia Pappas, Hui Zeng, Xiangjie Sun, Juan A. De La Cruz, Yasuko Hatta, Han Di
摘要中文翻译摘要:源自欧亚大陆的A6 A(H5N5)病毒进入北美,增加了北美高致病性禽流感病毒的遗传多样性,并提高了对人兽共患病风险的关注。在发生A(H5N5)病毒A/Washington/2148/2025致人死亡感染后,评估了该病毒在极化的人类支气管上皮细胞中的复制情况,并在雪貂模型中评估了其致病性、直接接触和呼吸道飞沫传播模型中的传播能力以及空气传播病毒释放情况,以了解大流行风险。A(H5N5)病毒在Calu-3细胞中于33°C和37°C均表现出强大的复制能力,其复制动力学和峰值滴度与当代B3.13和D1.1型A(H5N1)病毒相当。在雪貂中,A(H5N5)病毒在呼吸道中高效复制,扩散至非肺组织,并导致所有接种动物死亡。未观察到空气传播的传播,且空气样本中病毒的低水平、不频繁检测与在雪貂中无法通过空气传播的A(H5)病毒情况相似。在直接接触模型中,暴露4天后仅检测到有限的传播,并且接触动物中显示出下呼吸道复制的证据。这些结果表明,A(H5N5)病毒能够在气道上皮细胞系中实现强大的复制,并在雪貂中引起严重的全身感染和死亡率,但尚未获得支持空气传播的适应性。总体而言,这些结果突显了北美2.3.4.4b系A(H5Nx)病毒的多样性,并强调了对新出现病毒进行基因型对基因型评估的必要性,以理解其对公共卫生的风险。重要性:北美出现源自欧亚大陆的A6型高致病性禽流感 A(H5N5)病毒,增加了病毒的多样性,并引发了对人兽共患病和大流行风险的关注。在本研究中,我们使用极化的人类支气管上皮细胞和雪貂模型,评估了从首次报告的A(H5N5)亚型流感病毒感染病例中分离出的A/Washington/2148/2025 A(H5N5)病毒的复制动力学、发病机制和传播。该病毒在代表上呼吸道和下呼吸道温度的体外环境中高效复制,并导致接种雪貂出现致命的全身性疾病。在直接接触模型中,4天内仅观察到有限的传播。空气传播病毒的检测频率较低,且未导致空气传播的传播。这些发现表明,A(H5N5)病毒能够在哺乳动物细胞中实现强大的复制并引起严重疾病,但缺乏支持高效空气传播的适应性,从而为评估A6型流感病毒的大流行风险提供了信息。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估A(H5N5)病毒在北美出现后的流行病学风险,因其遗传多样性及潜在的跨物种溢出风险引发了对公共卫生影响的担忧。
设计与对象
该研究评估了A(H5N5)病毒在雪貂和极化的人类支气管上皮细胞中的复制、发病机制和传播,使用了首例人类A(H5N5)感染病例作为病毒的来源。
核心方法
A(H5N5)病毒在人类支气管上皮细胞中进行了复制测试,并评估了其致病性、直接接触和呼吸道飞沫模型中的传播能力以及在雪貂中的空气传播病毒脱落情况。
主要结果
A(H5N5)病毒在体外于上呼吸道和下呼吸道温度下均能高效复制,并在所有接种的雪貂中引起致命的全身性疾病。直接接触4天内观察到有限的传播,且空气传播病毒检测频率较低,未导致空气传播的传播。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,A(H5N5)病毒在雪貂中具有强大的复制能力和致病性,但尚未获得高效的空气传播适应性,这突显了其基于遗传起源的潜在大流行风险。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果有助于评估A6型流感病毒的疫情风险,并强调了对新出现的禽流感病毒进行评估的重要性,以确定其在哺乳动物中引起严重疾病和传播的潜力。
局限与证据强度
该研究突出了北美地区2.3.4.4b亚群A(H5Nx)病毒的异质性,并强调了需要进行基因型特异性评估以准确评估公共卫生风险,由于使用了豚鼠模型,研究结果具有中等置信度。

Risk assessment of avian influenza A(H5N5) virus from the first human case using the ferret model

Authors: Joanna Pulit-Penaloza, Jessica A. Belser, Nicole Brock, Troy J. Kieran, Claudia Pappas, Hui Zeng, Xiangjie Sun, Juan A. De La Cruz, Yasuko Hatta, Han Di
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT The incursion of Eurasian-origin genotype A6 A(H5N5) virus into North America expanded the genetic diversity among North American highly pathogenic avian influenza viruses and heightened concern about zoonotic risk. Following a fatal human infection with the A(H5N5) virus A/Washington/2148/2025, viral replication was assessed in polarized human bronchial epithelial cells, and pathogenicity, transmissibility in direct contact and respiratory droplet models, and airborne virus shedding were evaluated in ferrets to inform pandemic risk assessment. A(H5N5) displayed robust replication in Calu-3 cells at 33°C and 37°C, showing kinetics and peak titers comparable to those of contemporary genotype B3.13 and D1.1 A(H5N1) viruses. In ferrets, A(H5N5) replicated efficiently in the respiratory tract, disseminated to extrapulmonary tissues, and caused fatal disease in all inoculated animals. Airborne transmission was not observed, and infrequent, low-level detection of virus in air samples paralleled that of A(H5) viruses that are not transmissible via air in ferrets. In a direct contact model, limited transmission was detected within 4 days of exposure, with evidence of lower respiratory tract replication in contact animals. These findings indicate that the A(H5N5) virus has the capacity for robust replication in an airway epithelial cell line and can cause severe systemic infection and mortality in ferrets but has not acquired adaptations for airborne spread in mammals. Collectively, these results underscore heterogeneity among clade 2.3.4.4b A(H5Nx) viruses in North America and the need for genotype-by-genotype evaluation of newly emerged viruses to understand public health risk. IMPORTANCE The emergence of Eurasian-origin genotype A6 highly pathogenic avian influenza A(H5N5) virus in North America has increased viral diversity and raised concerns about zoonotic and pandemic risk. In this study, we evaluated the replication kinetics, pathogenesis, and transmission of A/Washington/2148/2025 A(H5N5) virus, which was isolated from the first reported human infection with this influenza virus subtype, using polarized human bronchial epithelial cells and the ferret model. The A(H5N5) virus replicated efficiently in vitro at temperatures representative of the upper and lower respiratory tracts and caused fatal systemic disease in inoculated ferrets. Limited transmission was observed during 4 days of direct contact. Airborne virus detection was infrequent and did not result in airborne transmission. These findings show that A(H5N5) virus can replicate robustly in mammalian cells and cause severe disease but lacks adaptations supporting efficient airborne spread, informing assessment of the pandemic risk posed by genotype A6 influenza viruses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess the pandemic risk of the A(H5N5) virus following its emergence in North America, as its genetic diversity and potential zoonotic risk raised concerns about public health implications.
Design and population
The study evaluated the replication, pathogenesis, and transmission of the A(H5N5) virus in ferrets and polarized human bronchial epithelial cells, using the first human case of A(H5N5) infection as the source of the virus.
Core methods
The A(H5N5) virus was tested for replication in human bronchial epithelial cells and for pathogenicity, transmissibility in direct contact and respiratory droplet models, and airborne virus shedding in ferrets.
Main results
The A(H5N5) virus replicated efficiently in vitro at both upper and lower respiratory tract temperatures and caused fatal systemic disease in all inoculated ferrets. Limited transmission was observed within 4 days of direct contact, and airborne virus detection was infrequent and did not result in airborne transmission.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that the A(H5N5) virus has the capacity for robust replication and severe disease in ferrets but has not acquired adaptations for efficient airborne spread, highlighting its potential pandemic risk based on its genetic origin.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results inform the assessment of pandemic risk posed by genotype A6 influenza viruses and emphasize the importance of evaluating newly emerged avian influenza viruses for their potential to cause severe disease and transmission in mammals.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study highlights the heterogeneity among clade 2.3.4.4b A(H5Nx) viruses in North America and underscores the need for genotype-specific evaluations to accurately assess public health risk, with a moderate confidence in the findings due to the use of a ferret model.

2.3.4.4b H5N1血凝素获得特定的人类呼吸道结合能力需要多个突变

Acquisition of specific human respiratory tract binding by 2.3.4.4b H5N1 hemagglutinins requires multiple mutations
作者： María Ríos Carrasco, Mafalda F. Guerreiro Cabana, Eszter Kovács, Zoé Ducarne, Cindy G. J. Cleypool, Geert-Jan Boons, Robert P. de Vries
摘要中文翻译摘要 有人提出,感染人类的​​牛源 2.3.4.4b 病毒 A/Texas/34 H5N1 (H5TX) 的血凝素仅需要一个 突变,即 Q226L,即可从结合禽类受体偏好转变为结合人型受体偏好。在这项研究中,我们检查了 H5TX Q226L 以及其他关键 突变 与人类气管切片的结合。我们的结论是,虽然 H5TX Q226L 可以结合人型受体,但该蛋白需要多个 突变 才能与人呼吸道组织结合。我们还报告了另一种 2.3.4.4b HA 突变体 H5FR Q226L(来自 A/duck/France/161108/16 H5N8)的受体结合特异性的变化,该变化与多碱基切割位点的存在相关。这项研究深入了解了 进化 在当前流行的 H5Nx 病毒中与人型受体结合的决定因素。它还强调使用其他方法(包括基于组织的方法以及合成聚糖)测试单个菌株的重要性。重要性 目前,H5N1 甲型流感病毒导致许多人畜共患 跨物种溢出 事件,从感染鸟类到其他哺乳动物,包括奶牛。尽管尚未观察到人传人的 传播,但已有多人被感染。这种 宿主 范围的扩展通常与病毒表面蛋白之一血凝素的变化有关,血凝素可以将其偏好从鸟类型受体转变为人类型受体。为了更好地了解当前流行的 H5N1 病毒在人类之间传播的潜力,我们评估了 Q226L 突变 与其他氨基酸取代相结合对与人类气管结合的影响。我们还研究了多碱基切割位点(高致病性流感病毒株中存在的特定基序)对受体结合特性的影响。这些发现提供了对受体结合在流感感染中的作用的深入了解。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在通过考察突变对受体结合特性的影响,探讨H5N1流感病毒在人类中传播的潜力。已有研究表明,感染人类的牛源性2.3.4.4b病毒A/Texas/34 H5N1(H5TX)的血凝素仅需一个突变,即Q226L,即可从结合禽类受体转变为结合人类受体。
设计与对象
本研究考察了H5TX Q226L以及其它关键突变对人类气管部分的结合能力。同时研究了多基切割位点,这一在高致病性流感病毒中特有的特定基序,对受体结合特性的影响。
核心方法
本研究评估了Q226L 突变与其他氨基酸替换组合对结合人类气管的影响。同时研究了多基切割位点对受体结合特性的影响。
主要结果
我们得出结论,尽管H5TX Q226L能够结合人类受体,但该蛋白要结合人类呼吸道组织需要不止一个突变。我们还报告了另一种2.3.4.4b HA突变体H5FR Q226L(来自A/duck/France/161108/16 H5N8)的受体结合特异性变化,该变化与多基切割位点的存在有关。
解释与创新
本研究提供了关于当前流行H5Nx病毒中进化向人类受体结合决定因素的见解。它强调了使用包括基于组织的方法在内的额外方法对单个菌株进行测试的重要性,同时结合合成糖类。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现提供了关于受体结合在流感感染中作用的见解。它们突显了进一步研究的必要性,以理解H5N1病毒在人类中传播的潜力。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据未报告具体研究设计的局限性。证据强度中等,因为研究结果基于受体结合实验和基于组织的方法,这些方法可能无法完全反映病毒传播在体内的复杂性。

Acquisition of specific human respiratory tract binding by 2.3.4.4b H5N1 hemagglutinins requires multiple mutations

Authors: María Ríos Carrasco, Mafalda F. Guerreiro Cabana, Eszter Kovács, Zoé Ducarne, Cindy G. J. Cleypool, Geert-Jan Boons, Robert P. de Vries
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT It has been suggested that the hemagglutinin of the human-infecting cattle-derived 2.3.4.4b virus A/Texas/34 H5N1 (H5TX) requires only one mutation, namely Q226L, to switch from binding avian-type to human-type receptor preference. In this study, we examined the binding of H5TX Q226L, along with other key mutations, to sections of human trachea. We conclude that, while H5TX Q226L can bind human-type receptors, more than a single mutation is required for this protein to bind to human respiratory tract tissue. We also report changes in receptor-binding specificity of another 2.3.4.4b HA mutant, H5FR Q226L (from A/duck/France/161108/16 H5N8), associated with the presence of a multibasic cleavage site. This study offers insight into the determinants of evolution toward human-type receptor binding in currently circulating H5Nx viruses. It also emphasizes the importance of testing individual strains using additional methods, including tissue-based approaches, alongside synthetic glycans. IMPORTANCE Currently, H5N1 influenza A viruses are responsible for numerous zoonotic spillover events, from infecting birds to other mammals, including dairy cattle. Although no human-to-human transmission has been observed, several people have been infected. This host range expansion is typically linked to changes in one of the viral surface proteins, hemagglutinin, which can switch its preference from avian-type to human-type receptors. To better understand the potential of the currently circulating H5N1 virus to transmit among humans, we evaluated the effects of the Q226L mutation, in combination with other amino acid substitutions, on binding to the human trachea. We also studied the effect of the multibasic cleavage site, a specific motif present in highly pathogenic influenza strains, on receptor-binding properties. These findings provide insight into the role of receptor binding in influenza infections.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the potential of H5N1 influenza viruses to transmit among humans by examining the effects of mutations on receptor-binding properties. It has been suggested that the hemagglutinin of the human-infecting cattle-derived 2.3.4.4b virus A/Texas/34 H5N1 (H5TX) requires only one mutation, namely Q226L, to switch from binding avian-type to human-type receptor preference.
Design and population
This study examined the binding of H5TX Q226L, along with other key mutations, to sections of human trachea. It also studied the effect of the multibasic cleavage site, a specific motif present in highly pathogenic influenza strains, on receptor-binding properties.
Core methods
The study evaluated the effects of the Q226L mutation, in combination with other amino acid substitutions, on binding to the human trachea. It also studied the effect of the multibasic cleavage site on receptor-binding properties.
Main results
We conclude that, while H5TX Q226L can bind human-type receptors, more than a single mutation is required for this protein to bind to human respiratory tract tissue. We also report changes in receptor-binding specificity of another 2.3.4.4b HA mutant, H5FR Q226L (from A/duck/France/161108/16 H5N8), associated with the presence of a multibasic cleavage site.
Interpretation and novelty
This study offers insight into the determinants of evolution toward human-type receptor binding in currently circulating H5Nx viruses. It emphasizes the importance of testing individual strains using additional methods, including tissue-based approaches, alongside synthetic glycans.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings provide insight into the role of receptor binding in influenza infections. They highlight the need for further research to understand the potential for human-to-human transmission of H5N1 viruses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations to the study design. The evidence strength is moderate, as the findings are based on receptor-binding assays and tissue-based approaches, which may not fully capture the complexity of viral transmission in vivo.

Comparison of Baloxavir-Based Combinations and Monotherapies for Treating Influenza A(H5N1) Clade 2.3.4.4b Virus Infection in Mice

查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估基于巴洛沙韦的联合用药和单药治疗对小鼠H5N1亚型2.3.4.4b病毒感染的疗效,因为抗病毒药物(A(H5N1))持续导致动物疾病和零星的人兽共患病。
设计与对象
本研究使用了一种致死性流感疾病的小鼠模型,其中6至8周龄的雌性BALB/c小鼠通过鼻腔途径感染了A/mink/Spain/3691–8_22VIR10586–10/2022病毒,剂量为5 LD50,接种体积为50 μL。研究在受控的实验室环境中进行。
核心方法
The study tested monotherapies and 2-drug combinations of oseltamivir phosphate (OSP), baloxavir acid (BXA), and molnupiravir (MPV). Mice were treated at 6 hours postinfection, with OSP and MPV administered orally twice daily for 5 days and BXA given once subcutaneously. Viral titers were quantified in lung, brain, and heart tissues using TCID50 assays at 4 and 6 dpi.
主要结果
在低剂量单药治疗中,OSP和MPV各提供了部分保护,存活率为2/7,而BXA的存活率则更高,为4/7。使用载体处理的小鼠出现了快速体重下降和统一死亡。将剂量提升至50 mg/kg的单药治疗改善了结果,但并未确保100%的保护。
解释与创新
基于巴洛沙韦的联合用药显示出更高的存活率和更少的病毒扩散,表明其在H5N1感染应对策略中可能具有潜在价值。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持使用基于巴洛沙韦的治疗方案来减轻H5N1感染的严重程度,这可能为未来抗病毒药物应对策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅限于小鼠模型,因此结果可能无法完全反映人类情况。证据强度中等,因为研究在受控环境中提供了直接观察,但缺乏更广泛的临床验证。

Comparison of Baloxavir-Based Combinations and Monotherapies for Treating Influenza A(H5N1) Clade 2.3.4.4b Virus Infection in Mice

Authors: Seong Cheol Min, Ju Ryeong Lee, Beom Kyu Kim, Ji-Hyun Park, Dong Gyu Lee, Gi Chan Lee, Se Hee An, Santosh Chokkakula, Aman Jain, Young Ki Choi

Volume 32, Number 10—October 2026 Dispatch Comparison of Baloxavir-Based Combinations and Monotherapies for Treating Influenza A(H5N1) Clade 2.3.4.4b Virus Infection in Mice Suggested citation for this article Abstract Highly pathogenic avian influenza A(H5N1) clade 2.3.4.4b virus continues to cause animal outbreaks and sporadic zoonotic infections. In a mouse model of lethal influenza disease, we compared oseltamivir, baloxavir, and molnupiravir monotherapies with 2-drug combinations. Baloxavir-based combinations improved survival, reduced lung viral loads, and prevented extrapulmonary dissemination, supporting H5N1 preparedness strategies. Highly pathogenic avian influenza A(H5N1) clade 2.3.4.4b virus continues to cause widespread outbreaks and sporadic zoonotic infections, underscoring the need to optimize antiviral strategies (1–3). Licensed influenza antiviral drugs can reduce disease severity but might be compromised in severe infections by high viral burdens, treatment delays, and treatment-emergent resistance, motivating evaluation of combination regimens (4–7). Recent H5N1 treatment studies using mouse models suggest antivi...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the efficacy of baloxavir-based combinations and monotherapies in treating influenza A(H5N1) clade 2.3.4.4b virus infection in mice, as highly pathogenic avian influenza A(H5N1) continues to cause animal outbreaks and sporadic zoonotic infections.
Design and population
The study used a mouse model of lethal influenza disease, where 6- to 8-week-old female BALB/c mice were intranasally challenged with A/mink/Spain/3691–8_22VIR10586–10/2022 at 5 LD50 in a 50-μL inoculum. The study was conducted in a controlled laboratory setting.
Core methods
The study tested monotherapies and 2-drug combinations of oseltamivir phosphate (OSP), baloxavir acid (BXA), and molnupiravir (MPV). Mice were treated at 6 hours postinfection, with OSP and MPV administered orally twice daily for 5 days and BXA given once subcutaneously. Viral titers were quantified in lung, brain, and heart tissues using TCID50 assays at 4 and 6 dpi.
Main results
In low-dose monotherapy, OSP and MPV provided partial protection with 2/7 survival each, while BXA had a higher survival rate of 4/7. Vehicle-treated mice experienced rapid weight loss and uniform death. Dose escalation to 50 mg/kg monotherapies improved outcomes but did not ensure 100% protection.
Interpretation and novelty
Baloxavir-based combinations showed improved survival and reduced viral dissemination, suggesting their potential value in preparedness strategies for H5N1 infections.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support the use of baloxavir-based regimens in mitigating the severity of H5N1 infections, which could inform future antiviral treatment strategies for avian influenza outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited to a mouse model, and results may not fully translate to human outcomes. The evidence is moderate, as the study provides direct observations in a controlled setting but lacks broader clinical validation.

迁徙鸟类中低致病性 禽流感 A 病毒的分子 监测 与部分遗传特征分析

Molecular Surveillance and Partial Genetic Characterization of Low-Pathogenic Avian Influenza A Viruses in Migrating Birds from Ukraine
作者： Anton Gerilovych, Ivan Rusev, Ihor Korovin, Anastasiia Rusanova, Gregory E. Glass
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
野生迁徙鸟类是流感 A 病毒(IAVs)的主要自然 储存宿主,它们在病毒的 进化 和全球传播中起着核心作用。
设计与对象
乌克兰位于黑海-地中海迁徙路线,使得在 野生鸟类 中检测 禽流感 病毒的 监测 在 One Health 框架下至关重要,尽管该地区的基因组数据仍然有限。
核心方法
Between October 2025 and January 2026, 604 cloacal and oropharyngeal swabs and environmental fecal samples were collected from migratory birds and mixed-species flocks in the Tuzlivski lymany National Nature Park and surrounding areas, together with samples from nearby domestic poultry. Influenza A virus was detected by real-time RT-PCR targeting the matrix gene, followed by H5/H7 screening, Oxford Nanopore sequencing of complete hemagglutinin genes, and phylogenetic analysis.
主要结果
在 604 份样本中,有 30 份检测到流感 A 病毒 RNA(4.97%),而所有样本均未检出 H5 和 H7 亚型。
解释与创新
完整血凝素序列揭示了三种低致病性亚型(H1、H4 和 H6),这些亚型在已建立的欧亚谱系中聚类,与当前在欧洲、亚洲和北美洲流行的禽流感病毒密切相关。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为乌克兰的 禽流感 监测 提供了重要的基因组基线,并展示了便携式纳米孔测序和整合 One Health 监测 在监测病毒多样性和支持新兴流感威胁早期检测中的价值。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告局限性或证据强度。

Molecular Surveillance and Partial Genetic Characterization of Low-Pathogenic Avian Influenza A Viruses in Migrating Birds from Ukraine

Authors: Anton Gerilovych, Ivan Rusev, Ihor Korovin, Anastasiia Rusanova, Gregory E. Glass
Original abstract (English)
Wild migratory birds are the principal natural reservoir of influenza A viruses (IAVs) and play a central role in their evolution and global dissemination. Ukraine lies along the Black Sea–Mediterranean Flyway, making surveillance of avian influenza viruses in wild birds essential within a One Health framework, although genomic data from the region remain limited. Between October 2025 and January 2026, 604 cloacal and oropharyngeal swabs and environmental fecal samples were collected from migratory birds and mixed-species flocks in the Tuzlivski lymany National Nature Park and surrounding areas, together with samples from nearby domestic poultry. Influenza A virus was detected by real-time RT-PCR targeting the matrix gene, followed by H5/H7 screening, Oxford Nanopore sequencing of complete hemagglutinin genes, and phylogenetic analysis. Influenza A virus RNA was identified in 30/604 samples (4.97%), whereas all samples were negative for H5 and H7 subtypes. Complete hemagglutinin sequences revealed three low-pathogenic subtypes (H1, H4, and H6) that clustered within established Eurasian lineages closely related to contemporary avian viruses circulating across Europe, Asia, and North America. No influenza A virus or Newcastle disease virus was detected in domestic poultry. These findings provide an important genomic baseline for avian influenza surveillance in Ukraine and demonstrate the value of portable nanopore sequencing and integrated One Health surveillance for monitoring viral diversity and supporting early detection of emerging influenza threats.

Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations or evidence strength.

延长给药间隔可增强自扩增mRNA 疫苗对大流行H5N1流感的持久性:一项随机、观察者盲法、剂量探索的I期研究

Extended dosing intervals enhance the durability of a self-amplifying mRNA vaccine against pandemic H5N1 influenza: a randomized, observer-blind, dose-finding Phase I study
作者： Antoinette A. Pragalos, Filippo Pacciarini, Hongfan Jin, Terri Hopkins, Ye Zhang, Roberto Bugarini, Xuexuan Liu, Brian M. Sullivan, Carole Verhoeven, Igor Smolenov
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
禽流感构成持续的大流行威胁,但传统疫苗面临生产延迟。自扩增mRNA(sa-mRNA)技术提供了潜在优势,包括快速开发、剂量节省以及增强且持久的免疫性。
设计与对象
健康成年人(N = 212)在4周或8周的间隔下接受两剂ARCT-2304(1.5、5或12 mcg)或对照/安慰剂。该研究是一项随机、观察者盲法、剂量探索的I期试验(NCT06602531)。
核心方法
主要结果评估了安全性与反应原性(招募/未招募/严重不良事件[AEs])以及免疫原性(第二剂后28天的HA抑制和NA酶联凝集素试验)。免疫原性结果通过来自先前支持H5N1 疫苗上市许可研究的血清进行基准比较。
主要结果
ARCT-2304耐受性良好,主要表现为轻度至中度、短暂且剂量依赖的招募不良事件,未发现安全性问题。两剂诱导了强效的HA和NA体液免疫应答。8周的给药间隔比4周的计划产生了更高的免疫应答幅度和更持久的免疫效果,第二剂后6至7个月,中和抗体和抗NA抗体仍显著升高。
解释与创新
该研究证明,延长给药间隔可以增强sa-mRNA 疫苗对H5N1流感的持久性,为大流行准备提供了一种有前景的策略。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持推进sa-mRNA平台用于大流行性流感的准备,特别是在需要快速疫苗开发和持久免疫的场景中。
局限与证据强度
该研究是一项I期试验,样本量有限,主要关注安全性和免疫原性,而非有效性,这可能限制其在更广泛人群或真实世界环境中的推广性。

Extended dosing intervals enhance the durability of a self-amplifying mRNA vaccine against pandemic H5N1 influenza: a randomized, observer-blind, dose-finding Phase I study

Authors: Antoinette A. Pragalos, Filippo Pacciarini, Hongfan Jin, Terri Hopkins, Ye Zhang, Roberto Bugarini, Xuexuan Liu, Brian M. Sullivan, Carole Verhoeven, Igor Smolenov
Original abstract (English)
Immunogenicity results were benchmarked using serum from a prior study supporting licensure of an H5N1 vaccine. These findings support advancing sa-mRNA platforms for pandemic influenza preparedness. Acknowledgements The authors are grateful to the study staff for assisting with study conduct, to the IQVIA Laboratories (Montreal, Canada, and NC, USA) and to Dr. Luc Gagnon for immunogenicity assays, and to CTI Clinical Research Organization for study conduct, study management, and statistical analysis. The authors thank Katie Boyd for program management; Joyce Simpauco for contributing to study oversight; Rose Sekulovich and Sophie Liu for strategic input on method development and validation, as well as laboratory testing oversight; Patrick Xin for data management oversight; Gregory Peters for investigational vaccine management; and Cindy Fisher for participating in discussions with regulatory authorities regarding the protocol and development plan. Abstract Avian influenza poses a persistent pandemic threat, yet traditional vaccines face production delays. Self-amplifying mRNA (sa-mRNA) technology offers potential advantages, including rapid development, dose sparing, and enhanced, durable immunity. We evaluated ARCT-2304, a bicistronic sa-mRNA vaccine encoding H5N1 hemagglutinin (HA) and neuraminidase (NA), in a Phase 1 randomized, observer-blind trial (NCT06602531). Healthy adults (N = 212) received two doses of ARCT-2304 (1.5, 5, or 12 mcg) or control/placebo on a 4- or 8-week schedule. Primary outcomes evaluated safety and reactogenicity (solicited/...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Avian influenza poses a persistent pandemic threat, yet traditional vaccines face production delays. Self-amplifying mRNA (sa-mRNA) technology offers potential advantages, including rapid development, dose sparing, and enhanced, durable immunity.
Design and population
Healthy adults (N = 212) received two doses of ARCT-2304 (1.5, 5, or 12 mcg) or control/placebo on a 4- or 8-week schedule. The study was a randomized, observer-blind, dose-finding Phase I trial (NCT06602531).
Core methods
Primary outcomes evaluated safety and reactogenicity (solicited/unsolicited/serious adverse events [AEs]) and immunogenicity (HA inhibition and NA enzyme-linked lectin assays 28 days post-second dose). Immunogenicity results were benchmarked using serum from a prior study supporting licensure of an H5N1 vaccine.
Main results
ARCT-2304 was well-tolerated, displaying mostly mild-to-moderate, transient, dose-dependent solicited AEs, with no safety concerns identified. Two doses elicited robust HA and NA humoral responses. An 8-week interval yielded higher magnitude and greater durability than a 4-week schedule, with neutralizing and anti-NA antibodies remaining significantly elevated 6–7 months post-second dose.
Interpretation and novelty
The study demonstrates that extended dosing intervals can enhance the durability of sa-mRNA vaccines against H5N1 influenza, offering a promising strategy for pandemic preparedness.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support advancing sa-mRNA platforms for pandemic influenza preparedness, particularly in scenarios requiring rapid vaccine development and durable immunity.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was a Phase I trial with a limited sample size, focusing on safety and immunogenicity rather than efficacy, which may limit generalizability to broader populations or real-world settings.

从分子进化到学校卫生室:挪威社区为基础的公共卫生护士(helsesykepleiere)在跨物种流感传播的一体化健康沟通中的作用

From molecular evolution to the school health room: the role of community-based public health nurses (helsesykepleiere) in one health communication on cross-species influenza transmission in Norway
作者： Jörn Klein
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在了解禽流感 A病毒,特别是HPAI A(H5N1) 2.3.4.4b亚型病毒,如何扩展其跨物种传播范围以及对人类健康的影响。它强调了将病毒学和流行病学证据转化为针对儿童和家庭的实用公共卫生沟通策略的空白。
设计与对象
本研究是一项综述,聚焦于当前关于跨物种传播的分子基础的文献,特别关注挪威的背景。它探讨了社区为基础的公共卫生护士在向儿童、青少年和家庭传递一体化健康沟通中的作用。
核心方法
研究方法包括综合现有文献中关于流感A病毒进化的分子机制、挪威监测系统的流行病学数据,以及针对儿童和家庭健康的已建立的风险沟通框架。
主要结果
综述记录了2.3.4.4b亚型禽流感病毒在哺乳动物中的反复跨物种溢出,并强调了与动物密切接触相关的低但非零的人类风险。同时,它也指出了青少年一体化健康素养方面的空白。
解释与创新
作者认为,挪威的公共卫生护士在连接病毒学研究与公共卫生实践方面具有独特的优势,提出了一个将人兽共患病流感科学转化为可操作家庭指导的新框架。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,利用公共卫生护士现有的接触网络可以改善社区中人兽共患病的沟通和防控准备,特别是对与动物有密切接触的儿童和家庭。
局限与证据强度
本研究基于现有文献的综述,未提供原创的实证数据,这限制了其结论的强度。然而,它提供了当前知识的全面综合,并指出了未来研究的关键领域。

From molecular evolution to the school health room: the role of community-based public health nurses (helsesykepleiere) in one health communication on cross-species influenza transmission in Norway

Authors: Jörn Klein
Original abstract (English)
Highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) A(H5N1) clade 2.3.4.4b viruses have undergone an unprecedented expansion of host range since 2021, moving from wild birds and poultry into dairy cattle, domestic cats, and other mammals, with sporadic human infections in Europe and North America ( Food and Agriculture Organization et al., 2025a ; Rolfes et al., 2025 ). This expansion is underpinned by well-characterised molecular mechanisms of influenza A virus evolution — antigenic drift, gene segment reassortment, and receptor-binding adaptation — that shape the frequency and direction of cross-species spillover ( McDonald et al., 2016 ; Steel and Lowen, 2017 ; Man et al., 2026 ). Norwegian wild-bird and mammalian surveillance data document recurrent incursions of clade 2.3.4.4b viruses, and international risk assessments continue to describe low, but non-zero, human risk concentrated among individuals with close animal contact ( World Health Organization, 2025–2026 ; Ytrehus et al ; European Food Safety Authority et al., 2025a ). Translating this fast-moving virological and epidemiological evidence into everyday protective behavior for children, adolescents, and families remains an underdeveloped link in pandemic preparedness, compounded by documented gaps in adolescent One Health literacy ( Zucca et al., 2021 ). This mini review synthesizes current literature on the molecular basis of influenza cross-species transmission, the Norwegian epidemiological and One Health governance context, and established child- and family-oriented risk-communication frameworks, to argue that Norway’s community-based public health nurses (helsesykepleiere) — who deliver near-universal, statutorily mandated contact with children and families through the helsestasjon- and skolehelsetjeneste — occupy a distinctive but underused position as One Health communication nodes ( Helsedirektoratet, 2021 ). I outline a practical three-tier framework for translating zoonotic-influenza science into age-appropriate household guidance, critically appraise the feasibility, workload, training, and governance implications of this role, and identify priority gaps for future empirical and implementation research.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the need to understand how avian influenza A viruses, particularly HPAI A(H5N1) clade 2.3.4.4b, have expanded their host range and the implications for human health. It highlights the gap in translating virological and epidemiological evidence into practical public health communication strategies for children and families.
Design and population
The study is a mini review that synthesises current literature on avian influenza cross-species transmission, focusing on the Norwegian context. It examines the role of community-based public health nurses in delivering One Health communication to children, adolescents, and families.
Core methods
The methods involve a synthesis of existing literature on molecular mechanisms of influenza A virus evolution, epidemiological data from Norwegian surveillance systems, and established risk-communication frameworks for child and family health.
Main results
The review documents recurrent incursions of clade 2.3.4.4b avian influenza viruses into mammals and highlights the low but non-zero human risk associated with close animal contact. It also identifies gaps in adolescent One Health literacy.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that Norwegian public health nurses are uniquely positioned to bridge the gap between virological research and public health practice, offering a novel framework for translating zoonotic influenza science into actionable household guidance.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that leveraging the existing contact networks of public health nurses could improve risk communication and preparedness for avian influenza in communities, particularly for children and families with close animal interactions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on a review of existing literature and does not present original empirical data, which limits the strength of its conclusions. However, it provides a comprehensive synthesis of current knowledge and highlights critical areas for future research.

温度依赖的Volterra积分微分模型对禽流感的传播动力学分析与数值解:通过增强型Adomian分解法结合饱和发病率和环境反馈

Analytical–Numerical Solution of a Temperature-Dependent Volterra Integro-Differential Model for Avian Influenza Transmission with Saturated Incidence and Environmental Feedback Via an Enhanced Adomian Decomposition Method
作者： Valentine Ejes
摘要中文翻译本研究提出了一种温度依赖的Volterra积分微分模型的分析-数值研究,用于分析禽流感的传播动力学,该模型结合了饱和发病率和环境反馈。该模型同时捕捉了鸟类之间的直接传播以及通过环境病毒载量的间接传播,并通过指数核函数考虑了记忆效应。传播过程使用非线性饱和发病率函数进行建模,以反映现实中的接触限制,同时假设环境病毒衰减明确依赖于温度。采用增强型Adomian分解法(EADM)来求解非线性系统的大致解析解。该方法有效处理了耦合积分微分结构和非线性交互项。为了验证解析解的准确性和收敛性,使用四阶龙格-库塔(RK4)方法进行了数值模拟。EADM与RK4解之间表现出高度一致,证实了所提出方法的可靠性和高效性。此外,利用下一代矩阵方法推导了基本再生数(Ro),揭示了直接和环境传播途径对传播的贡献。稳定性分析表明,当Ro < 1时,无病平衡点是局部渐近稳定的。研究还表明,温度对病毒衰减的影响在降低感染持续性方面起着重要作用,突显了其在疾病控制策略中的重要性。结果表明,结合环境记忆、温度效应和非线性发病率能够提供一个更现实的框架来理解禽流感的动力学,而增强型Adomian分解法为求解复杂的流行病学模型提供了一种高效的解析工具。

问题与背景

设计与对象
该模型同时捕捉了鸟类之间的直接传播以及通过环境病毒载量的间接传播,并通过指数核函数考虑了记忆效应。
核心方法

Analytical–Numerical Solution of a Temperature-Dependent Volterra Integro-Differential Model for Avian Influenza Transmission with Saturated Incidence and Environmental Feedback Via an Enhanced Adomian Decomposition Method

Authors: Valentine Ejes
Original abstract (English)
This study presents an analytical–numerical investigation of a temperature-dependent Volterra integro-differential model for the transmission dynamics of avian influenza incorporating saturated incidence and environmental feedback. The model captures both direct transmission among birds and indirect transmission through environmental viral load, while accounting for memory effects via an exponential kernel. The transmission process is modeled using a nonlinear saturated incidence function to reflect realistic contact limitations, and the environmental viral decay is assumed to depend explicitly on temperature. An enhanced Adomian Decomposition Method (EADM) is employed to derive approximate analytical solutions of the nonlinear system. The method effectively handles the coupled integro-differential structure and nonlinear interaction terms. To validate the accuracy and convergence of the analytical solution, numerical simulations are performed using the fourth-order Runge–Kutta (RK4) method. A close agreement between the EADM and RK4 solutions is observed, confirming the reliability and efficiency of the proposed approach. Furthermore, the basic reproduction number (Ro) is derived using the next-generation matrix method, revealing contributions from both direct and environmental transmission pathways. Stability analysis shows that the disease-free equilibrium is locally asymptotically stable when ( Ro 1). The influence of temperature on viral decay is shown to play a significant role in reducing infection persistence, highlighting its importance in disease control strategies. The results demonstrate that incorporating environmental memory, temperature effects, and nonlinear incidence provides a more realistic framework for understanding avian influenza dynamics, and the enhanced Adomian Decomposition Method offers an efficient analytical tool for solving complex epidemiological models.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study presents an analytical–numerical investigation of a temperature-dependent Volterra integro-differential model for the transmission dynamics of avian influenza incorporating saturated incidence and environmental feedback.
Design and population
The model captures both direct transmission among birds and indirect transmission through environmental viral load, while accounting for memory effects via an exponential kernel.
Core methods
The transmission process is modeled using a nonlinear saturated incidence function to reflect realistic contact limitations, and the environmental viral decay is assumed to depend explicitly on temperature.
Main results
A close agreement between the EADM and RK4 solutions is observed, confirming the reliability and efficiency of the proposed approach.
Interpretation and novelty
The results demonstrate that incorporating environmental memory, temperature effects, and nonlinear incidence provides a more realistic framework for understanding avian influenza dynamics, and the enhanced Adomian Decomposition Method offers an efficient analytical tool for solving complex epidemiological models.
Scientific and public-health significance
The influence of temperature on viral decay is shown to play a significant role in reducing infection persistence, highlighting its importance in disease control strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations or evidence strength.
